  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Leo: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. Carol was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive to target her. I'm suspicious of Frank because he seemed too calm and didn't show much emotion when Carol was killed. Let's discuss and vote carefully.
  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. We were the ones who actually did the killing, so I think we should be careful about pointing fingers. Let's keep our heads clear and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Grace: Eve's comment is concerning. If she and Noah were the ones who killed Carol, why would she admit it so openly? That seems like a strong clue that she might be a werewolf trying to throw us off. I think we should focus on Eve and Noah as suspects.
  Uma: I investigated Frank last night, and he is a Villager. While his calmness might be suspicious, the investigation confirms he's not a werewolf. However, Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly unusual and raises serious doubts about her loyalty. We need to scrutinize her more closely. Let's not jump to conclusions about Frank but consider Eve and Noah as potential threats.
  Iris: Uma's investigation confirms Frank is a Villager, so we can rule him out. However, Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is extremely suspicious—werewolves wouldn’t openly admit to murder. This makes Eve and Noah the top suspects. Let’s focus our discussion and vote on them.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Leo, Eve, Grace, Uma, Iris, Frank, Victor, Bob, Tara, Noah, Paul

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Leo: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. Carol was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive to target her. I'm suspicious of Frank because he seemed too calm and didn't show much emotion when Carol was killed. Let's discuss and vote carefully.
  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. We were the ones who actually did the killing, so I think we should be careful about pointing fingers. Let's keep our heads clear and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Grace: Eve's comment is concerning. If she and Noah were the ones who killed Carol, why would she admit it so openly? That seems like a strong clue that she might be a werewolf trying to throw us off. I think we should focus on Eve and Noah as suspects.
  Uma: I investigated Frank last night, and he is a Villager. While his calmness might be suspicious, the investigation confirms he's not a werewolf. However, Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly unusual and raises serious doubts about her loyalty. We need to scrutinize her more closely. Let's not jump to conclusions about Frank but consider Eve and Noah as potential threats.
  Iris: Uma's investigation confirms Frank is a Villager, so we can rule him out. However, Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is extremely suspicious—werewolves wouldn’t openly admit to murder. This makes Eve and Noah the top suspects. Let’s focus our discussion and vote on them.
  Frank: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a major red flag. Werewolves would never openly admit to murder, especially in front of the group. Her confession seems like a trap to divert suspicion. I believe Eve and Noah are the most likely werewolves and should be the focus of our vote.

[ACTION={'statement': "Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a major red flag. Werewolves would never openly admit to murder, especially in front of the group. === Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Leo, Eve, Grace, Uma, Iris, Frank, Victor, Bob, Tara, Noah, Paul

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Leo: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. Carol was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive to target her. I'm suspicious of Frank because he seemed too calm and didn't show much emotion when Carol was killed. Let's discuss and vote carefully.
  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. We were the ones who actually did the killing, so I think we should be careful about pointing fingers. Let's keep our heads clear and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Grace: Eve's comment is concerning. If she and Noah were the ones who killed Carol, why would she admit it so openly? That seems like a strong clue that she might be a werewolf trying to throw us off. I think we should focus on Eve and Noah as suspects.
  Uma: I investigated Frank last night, and he is a Villager. While his calmness might be suspicious, the investigation confirms he's not a werewolf. However, Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly unusual and raises serious doubts about her loyalty. We need to scrutinize her more closely. Let's not jump to conclusions about Frank but consider Eve and Noah as potential threats.
  Iris: Uma's investigation confirms Frank is a Villager, so we can rule him out. However, Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is extremely suspicious—werewolves wouldn’t openly admit to murder. This makes Eve and Noah the top suspects. Let’s focus our discussion and vote on them.
  Frank: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.
  Victor: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a major red flag. Werewolves would never openly admit to murder, especially in front of the group. Her confession seems like a trap to divert suspicion. I believe Eve and Noah are the most likely werewolves and should be the focus of our vote.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: Eve's admission of involvement in Carol's death is extremely suspicious. Werewolves would never confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly suggests she is a werewolf. Noah, being her accomplice in her own words, should also be considered a suspect. We need to focus our vote on them.

[ACTION={'statement': "Eve's admission of involvement in Carol's death is extremely suspicious. Werewolves would never confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly suggests she is a werewolf.   Frank: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.

  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. We were the ones who actually did the killing, so I think we should be careful about pointing fingers. Let's keep our heads clear and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Grace: Eve's comment is concerning. If she and Noah were the ones who killed Carol, why would she admit it so openly? That seems like a strong clue that she might be a werewolf trying to throw us off. I think we should focus on Eve and Noah as suspects.
  Uma: I investigated Frank last night, and he is a Villager. While his calmness might be suspicious, the investigation confirms he's not a werewolf. However, Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly unusual and raises serious doubts about her loyalty. We need to scrutinize her more closely. Let's not jump to conclusions about Frank but consider Eve and Noah as potential threats.

  Frank: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.
  Victor: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a major red flag. Werewolves would never openly admit to murder, especially in front of the group. Her confession seems like a trap to divert suspicion. I believe Eve and Noah are the most likely werewolves and should be the focus of our vote.
  Bob: Eve's admission of involvement in Carol's death is extremely suspicious. Werewolves would never confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly suggests she is a werewolf. Noah, being her accomplice in her own words, should also be considered a suspect. We need to focus our vote on them.
  Tara: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is highly suspicious—werewolves would never confess to murder. Her openness raises serious doubts about her loyalty. I believe we should focus on Eve and Noah as the top suspects and vote accordingly.
  Noah: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a dangerous move. Werewolves would never confess to such an act in front of everyone—it's a clear sign of manipulation. I believe Eve is trying to divert suspicion, and Noah is likely her accomplice. We need to vote them out.
  Paul: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is a major red flag. Werewolves would never confess to such an act in front of the group—it's a clear attempt to manipulate the villagers.   Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Eve (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Leo: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. Carol was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive to target her. I'm suspicious of Frank because he seemed too calm and didn't show much emotion when Carol was killed. Let's discuss and vote carefully.
  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. We were the ones who actually did the killing, so I think we should be careful about pointing fingers. Let's keep our heads clear and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Grace: Eve's comment is concerning. If she and Noah were the ones who killed Carol, why would she admit it so openly? That seems like a strong clue that she might be a werewolf trying to throw us off. I think we should focus on Eve and Noah as suspects.
  Uma: I investigated Frank last night, and he is a Villager. While his calmness might be suspicious, the investigation confirms he's not a werewolf. However, Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly unusual and raises serious doubts about her loyalty. We need to scrutinize her more closely. Let's not jump to conclusions about Frank but consider Eve and Noah as potential threats.
  Iris: Uma's investigation confirms Frank is a Villager, so we can rule him out. However, Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is extremely suspicious—werewolves wouldn’t openly admit to murder. This makes Eve and Noah the top suspects. Let’s focus our discussion and vote on them.
  Frank: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.
  Victor: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a major red flag. Werewolves would never openly admit to murder, especially in front of the group. Her confession seems like a trap to divert suspicion. I believe Eve and Noah are the most likely werewolves and should be the focus of our vote.
  Bob: Eve's admission of involvement in Carol's death is extremely suspicious. Werewolves would never confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly suggests she is a werewolf. Noah, being her accomplice in her own words, should also be considered a suspect. We need to focus our vote on them.
  Tara: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is highly suspicious—werewolves would never confess to murder. Her openness raises serious doubts about her loyalty. I believe we should focus on Eve and Noah as the top suspects and vote accordingly.
  Noah: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a dangerous move. Werewolves would never confess to such an act in front of everyone—it's a clear sign of manipulation. I believe Eve is trying to divert suspicion, and Noah is likely her accomplice. We need to vote them out.
  Paul: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is a major red flag. Werewolves would never confess to such an act in front of the group—it's a clear attempt to manipulate the villagers. Noah, as her alleged accomplice, should also be under scrutiny. I believe we should focus our vote on Eve and Noah.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Leo: Eve's open admission of killing Carol is extremely suspicious—werewolves would never confess to murder in front of the group. This makes her a prime suspect, and Noah, who she implicated, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.
  Grace: Eve's confession is a clear red flag—werewolves don’t admit to killing in front of the group. Her behavior strongly suggests she’s a werewolf, and Noah, as her alleged accomplice, should also be scrutinized. We need to vote them out to protect the village.

  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. === Werewolf — Day 6 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (6): Leo, Grace, Iris, Victor, Noah, Paul

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Eve (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Uma (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Bob (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 5: Frank (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Leo: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. Carol was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive to target her. I'm suspicious of Frank because he seemed too calm and didn't show much emotion when Carol was killed. Let's discuss and vote carefully.
  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. We were the ones who actually did the killing, so I think we should be careful about pointing fingers. Let's keep our heads clear and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Grace: Eve's comment is concerning. If she and Noah were the ones who killed Carol, why would she admit it so openly? That seems like a strong clue that she might be a werewolf trying to throw us off. I think we should focus on Eve and Noah as suspects.
  Uma: I investigated Frank last night, and he is a Villager. While his calmness might be suspicious, the investigation confirms he's not a werewolf. However, Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly unusual and raises serious doubts about her loyalty. We need to scrutinize her more closely. Let's not jump to conclusions about Frank but consider Eve and Noah as potential threats.
  Iris: Uma's investigation confirms Frank is a Villager, so we can rule him out. However, Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is extremely suspicious—werewolves wouldn’t openly admit to murder. This makes Eve and Noah the top suspects. Let’s focus our discussion and vote on them.
  Frank: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.
  Victor: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a major red flag. Werewolves would never openly admit to murder, especially in front of the group. Her confession seems like a trap to divert suspicion. I believe Eve and Noah are the most likely werewolves and should be the focus of our vote.

You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

[SYSTEM]
You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

=== Werewolf — Day 7 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (5): Leo, Grace, Iris, Noah, Paul

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Eve (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Uma (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Bob (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 5: Frank (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 6: Victor (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Leo: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. Carol was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive to target her. I'm suspicious of Frank because he seemed too calm and didn't show much emotion when Carol was killed. Let's discuss and vote carefully.
  Eve: I think Frank's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Noah and I were the only ones who didn't react strongly when Carol was killed. We were the ones who actually did the killing, so I think we should be careful about pointing fingers. Let's keep our heads clear and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Grace: Eve's comment is concerning. If she and Noah were the ones who killed Carol, why would she admit it so openly? That seems like a strong clue that she might be a werewolf trying to throw us off. I think we should focus on Eve and Noah as suspects.
  Uma: I investigated Frank last night, and he is a Villager. While his calmness might be suspicious, the investigation confirms he's not a werewolf. However, Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly unusual and raises serious doubts about her loyalty. We need to scrutinize her more closely. Let's not jump to conclusions about Frank but consider Eve and Noah as potential threats.
  Iris: Uma's investigation confirms Frank is a Villager, so we can rule him out. However, Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is extremely suspicious—werewolves wouldn’t openly admit to murder. This makes Eve and Noah the top suspects. Let’s focus our discussion and vote on them.
  Frank: Eve's admission of being involved in Carol's death is highly suspicious. Werewolves wouldn’t confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly indicates she might be a werewolf. Noah, who she paired with, should also be under scrutiny. We need to vote carefully and eliminate one of them.
  Victor: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a major red flag. Werewolves would never openly admit to murder, especially in front of the group. Her confession seems like a trap to divert suspicion. I believe Eve and Noah are the most likely werewolves and should be the focus of our vote.
  Bob: Eve's admission of involvement in Carol's death is extremely suspicious. Werewolves would never confess to such an act, so her behavior strongly suggests she is a werewolf. Noah, being her accomplice in her own words, should also be considered a suspect. We need to focus our vote on them.
  Tara: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is highly suspicious—werewolves would never confess to murder. Her openness raises serious doubts about her loyalty. I believe we should focus on Eve and Noah as the top suspects and vote accordingly.
  Noah: Eve's claim that she and Noah killed Carol is a dangerous move. Werewolves would never confess to such an act in front of everyone—it's a clear sign of manipulation. I believe Eve is trying to divert suspicion, and Noah is likely her accomplice. We need to vote them out.
